Outrank batches\n// are small; a few MB is generous headroom for a legitimate publish.\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 MB\n// Cap the number of articles processed in a single delivery so one request\n// cannot tie up the function for an unbounded number of serial DB round-trips.\nconst MAX_ARTICLES = 100;\n\ntype OutrankArticle = {\n  id?: string;\n  title?: string;\n  content_markdown?: string;\n  content_html?: string;\n  meta_description?: string;\n  description?: string;\n  created_at?: string;\n  published_at?: string;\n  image_url?: string;\n  image_alt?: string;\n  slug?: string;\n  tags?: string[];\n  url?: string;\n};\n\ntype OutrankPayload = {\n  event_type?: string;\n  timestamp?: string;\n  data?: { articles?: OutrankArticle[] };\n};\n\n/**\n * Verify a per-request HMAC-SHA256 signature over the raw body. Returns false\n * when no signing secret is configured (so the caller can fall back to the\n * static bearer token).\n */\nfunction isSignatureValid(rawBody: string, header: string | null): boolean {\n  const secret = env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET;\n  if (!secret || !header) return false;\n\n  // Accept either a bare hex digest or a `sha256=<hex>` prefixed value.\n  const provided = header.startsWith(\"sha256=\")\n    ? header.slice(\"sha256=\".length).trim()\n    : header.trim();\n\n  const expected = createHmac(\"sha256\", secret).update(rawBody).digest(\"hex\");\n\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  const providedBuf = encoder.encode(provided.toLowerCase());\n  const expectedBuf = encoder.encode(expected);\n  if (providedBuf.length !== expectedBuf.length) return false;\n  return timingSafeEqual(providedBuf, expectedBuf);\n}\n\nfunction isBearerAuthorized(req: NextRequest): boolean {\n  const expected = env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN;\n  if (!expected) return false;\n  const header = req.headers.get(\"authorization\");\n  if (!header || !header.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) return false;\n  return timingSafeEqualSecret(header.slice(\"Bearer \".length).trim(), expected);\n}\n\nfunction slugify(input: string): string {\n  return input\n    .toString()\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .normalize(\"NFKD\")\n    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, \"\")\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, \"\")\n    .slice(0, 120);\n}\n\nconst LOCALE = \"en\";\n\n/**\n * Return true when `slug` is free within the locale, i.e. not already claimed\n * by a DIFFERENT external article. (A row owned by `externalId` itself is fine\n * — the upsert resolves that on the externalId conflict target.)\n */\nasync function isSlugFree(slug: string, externalId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const existing = await db\n    .select({ id: blogPosts.id })\n    .from(blogPosts)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(blogPosts.locale, LOCALE),\n        eq(blogPosts.slug, slug),\n        ne(blogPosts.externalId, externalId),\n      ),\n    )\n    .limit(1);\n  return existing.length === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve a slug that is unique within the locale. If the desired slug is\n * already taken by a DIFFERENT external article, append a suffix derived from\n * the external id so the new article still persists instead of throwing a\n * (locale, slug) unique violation. The first candidate is verified too — and if\n * it is also taken (e.g. duplicate-title articles whose external ids share the\n * leading 8 alphanumerics) we widen the suffix and finally fall back to the\n * full external id, which is globally unique, so a free slug is guaranteed.\n */\nasync function resolveUniqueSlug(\n  desired: string,\n  externalId: string,\n): Promise<string> {\n  if (await isSlugFree(desired, externalId)) return desired;\n\n  const sanitizedId = externalId.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, \"\").toLowerCase();\n\n  // Try progressively longer slices of the external id, then the full id, so\n  // articles that collide on a short shared prefix still resolve to distinct\n  // slugs. Each candidate is verified before use.\n  const suffixLengths = [8, 16, sanitizedId.length];\n  for (const len of suffixLengths) {\n    const suffix = sanitizedId.slice(0, len);\n    if (!suffix) continue;\n    const base = desired.slice(0, 120 - (suffix.length + 1));\n    const candidate = `${base}-${suffix}`;\n    if (await isSlugFree(candidate, externalId)) return candidate;\n  }\n\n  // Last resort: a timestamp keeps the slug unique even when the external id\n  // alone somehow still collides, so the article persists rather than being\n  // dropped on a unique violation.\n  const suffix = `${sanitizedId || \"post\"}-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;\n  const base = desired.slice(0, 120 - (suffix.length + 1));\n  return `${base}-${suffix}`;\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(value: string): string {\n  return value\n    .replace(/&/g, \"&amp;\")\n    .replace(/</g, \"&lt;\")\n    .replace(/>/g, \"&gt;\")\n    .replace(/\"/g, \"&quot;\")\n    .replace(/'/g, \"&#39;\");\n}\n\nfunction markdownToHtml(markdown: string): string {\n  return markdown\n    .trim()\n    .split(/\\n{2,}/)\n    .map((block) => {\n      const text = escapeHtml(block.trim());\n      if (!text) return \"\";\n\n      const heading = text.match(/^(#{1,3})\\s+(.+)$/);","numLines":180,"startLine":1,"totalLines":431}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6bf63967-9bbd-4ba7-98f6-674b54713ee2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

value: AuthContext }\n20\t  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n21\t\n22\t// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\n23\tfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n24\t  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n25\t  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n26\t}\n27\t\n28\tasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n29\t  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n30\t  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n31\t  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n32\t\n33\t  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n34\t\n35\t  try {\n36\t    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n37\t      method: 'POST',\n38\t      headers: {\n39\t        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n40\t      },\n41\t      body: JSON.stringify({\n42\t        license_key: licenseKey,\n43\t        include_credits: true,\n44\t      }),\n45\t      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n46\t    });\n47\t\n48\t    const responseText = await response.text();\n49\t    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n50\t\n51\t    try {\n52\t      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n53\t    } catch {\n54\t      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n58\t    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n59\t\n60\t    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n61\t\n62\t    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n63\t    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n64\t    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n65\t    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n66\t    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n67\t    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n68\t    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n69\t    // the licensing API on every call.\n70\t    if (response.status >= 500) {\n71\t      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n72\t      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n73\t    }\n74\t\n75\t    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n76\t      isValid,\n77\t      credits,\n78\t      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n79\t    });\n80\t\n81\t    return { isValid, credits };\n82\t  } catch (error) {\n83\t    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n84\t    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n85\t    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n86\t    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n87\t    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n88\t      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n89\t    } else {\n90\t      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n91\t    }\n92\t    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n93\t  }\n94\t}\n95\t\n96\texport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n97\t  const { licenseKey } = input;\n98\t\n99\t  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n100\t  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n101\t  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n102\t  if (!licenseKey) {\n103\t    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n104\t    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n105\t  }\n106\t\n107\t  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n108\t\n109\t  if (!forceRefresh) {\n110\t    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n111\t    if (cached) {\n112\t      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n113\t      if (!cached.isValid) {\n114\t        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n115\t      }\n116\t      return {\n117\t        ok: AUTHENTICATION HELPERS\n// Validates license keys and device trial identifiers\n\nimport { DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { cacheLicense, getCachedLicense } from '../lib/redis';\nimport { invalidLicenseResponse, licenseRequiredResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n\nexport interface AuthContext {\n  identifier: string; // license key\n  credits: number;\n  licenseKey: string;\n}\n\nexport interface AuthInput {\n  licenseKey?: string;\n}\n\nexport type AuthResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: AuthContext }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\n// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\nfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n    } catch {\n      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n    }\n\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n\n    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n\n    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n    // the licensing API on every call.\n    if (response.status >= 500) {\n      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n    }\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { isValid, credits };\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n    } else {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n    }\n    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n  const { licenseKey } = input;\n\n  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  if (!forceRefresh) {\n    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n      if (!cached.isValid) {\n        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n      }\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        value: {\n          identifier: licenseKey,\n          credits: cached.credits,\n          licenseKey,\n        },\n      };\n    }\n    console.log(`[Auth] Cache MISS for ${maskedKey}, calling API...`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`[Auth] Force refresh for ${maskedKey}, bypassing cache...`);\n  }\n\n  const validation = await validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey);\n  if (!validation.isValid) {\n    console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is INVALID`);\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n  }\n\n  console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is VALID with ${validation.credits} credits`);\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    value: {\n      identifier: licenseKey,\n      credits: validation.credits,\n      licenseKey,\n    },\n  };\n}\n","numLines":146,"startLine":1,"totalLines":146}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d1664f0d-34e2-4c8d-a2ec-3240b49fceeb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The bearer token, by contrast, is independent of the\n  // payload — so when HMAC signing is disabled (the normal direct-Outrank\n  // setup) reject a bad bearer token BEFORE allocating the body, instead of\n  // buffering an unsigned, unauthenticated request.\n  const signature =\n    req.headers.get(\"x-outrank-signature\") ??\n    req.headers.get(\"outrank-signature\");\n  const signingEnabled = Boolean(env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET);\n  if (!signingEnabled && !isBearerAuthorized(req)) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Invalid access token\" },\n      { status: 401 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Read the raw body once so we can both size-check it and verify an HMAC\n  // signature over the exact bytes.\n  let rawBody: string;\n  try {\n    rawBody = await req.text();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid body\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  if (Buffer.byteLength(rawBody, \"utf8\") > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Payload too large\" }, { status: 413 });\n  }\n\n  // When signing is enabled, prefer HMAC signature verification but still fall\n  // back to the static bearer token for callers that have not migrated to\n  // signing. (When signing is disabled the bearer token was already verified\n  // above before the body was buffered.)\n  if (signingEnabled) {\n    const authorized =\n      isSignatureValid(rawBody, signature) || isBearerAuthorized(req);\n    if (!authorized) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"Invalid access token\" },\n        { status: 401 },\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  let payload: OutrankPayload;\n  try {\n    payload = JSON.parse(rawBody) as OutrankPayload;\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid JSON\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  if (payload.event_type !== \"publish_articles\") {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: `Unsupported event_type: ${payload.event_type}` },\n      { status: 400 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const articles = payload.data?.articles;\n  if (!Array.isArray(articles) || articles.length === 0) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"No articles in payload\" },\n      { status: 400 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (articles.length > MAX_ARTICLES) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: `Too many articles: ${articles.length} (max ${MAX_ARTICLES})`,\n      },\n      { status: 413 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const processed: Array<{ id: string; slug: string }> = [];","numLines":80,"startLine":220,"totalLines":431}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"34b927a8-9dee-47bd-a394-14eae1573031","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\nimport Stripe from \"stripe\";\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport {\n  handleLicensePurchase,\n  handleCreditPurchase,\n  handleChargeRefunded,\n} from \"@/lib/services/stripe-webhook\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Webhook Handler\n *\n * Handles Stripe webhook events for HyperWhisper purchases.\n * Primary event: checkout.session.completed\n *\n * SUPPORTED PURCHASE TYPES:\n * - \"license\": One-time license purchase\n * - \"credits\": Credit pack purchase (adds to Stripe Billing Meter)\n *\n * SECURITY:\n * - Verifies webhook signature using STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET\n * - Uses raw body for signature verification (required by Stripe)\n */\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const body = await req.text();\n  const signature = req.headers.get(\"stripe-signature\");\n\n  if (!signature) {\n    console.error(\"Stripe webhook: No signature header\");\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"No signature\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  const webhookSecret = process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET;\n  if (!webhookSecret) {\n    console.error(\"Stripe webhook: STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET not configured\");\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Webhook secret not configured\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let event: Stripe.Event;\n\n  // Verify webhook signature\n  try {\n    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(body, signature, webhookSecret);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\n      \"Stripe webhook signature verification failed:\",\n      err instanceof Error ? err.message : err\n    );\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Webhook signature verification failed\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Stripe webhook received: ${event.type}`);\n\n  // Handle checkout session lifecycle events\n=== ws deepgram 165-200\n  c.set('wsClientIP', clientIP);\n\n  return next();\n}\n\nexport const wsStreamingRoute = upgradeWebSocket((c) => {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const auth = c.get('wsAuth');\n  const clientIP = c.get('wsClientIP');\n  const url = new URL(c.req.url);\n  const language = url.searchParams.get('language') || undefined;\n  const vocabulary = url.searchParams.get('vocabulary') || undefined;\n  const apiKey = process.env.DEEPGRAM_API_KEY || '';\n\n  let totalDurationSeconds = 0;\n  let bytesReceived = 0;\n  let deepgramWs: WebSocket | null = null;\n  let sessionEnded = false;\n  let clientSocket: WSContext | null = null;\n  let pingInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\n\n  const dgUrl = buildDeepgramUrl(language, vocabulary);\n\n  async function endSession(): Promise<void> {\n    if (sessionEnded) return;\n    sessionEnded = true;\n\n    if (pingInterval) {\n      clearInterval(pingInterval);\n      pingInterval = null;\n    }\n\n    const costUsd = computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds);\n    const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n    if (clientSocket) {\n      sendToClient(clientSocket, {\n        type: 'session_complete',\n        duration_seconds: totalDurationSeconds,\n Dedupe by the stable Polar license-key id (casing-independent).\n44\t    // findAccountByKey is a case-sensitive exact match, so a different casing\n45\t    // or whitespace variant of an already-imported key misses the DB lookup\n46\t    // and reaches this fallback. Without this guard each variant would insert a\n47\t    // fresh license row and grant another 5000 credits. polarResult.id is the\n48\t    // canonical resource id regardless of how the input key was cased.\n49\t    const alreadyImported = await findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(polarResult.id);\n50\t    if (alreadyImported) {\n51\t      return { success: true, licenseId: alreadyImported.id };\n52\t    }\n53\t\n54\t    // 3. Get customer email - required for import\n55\t    const email = polarResult.customer?.email;\n56\t    if (!email) {\n57\t      return { success: false, error: \"License has no associated email\" };\n58\t    }\n59\t\n60\t    // 4. Get or create user\n61\t    const user = await getOrCreateUser(email, {\n62\t      name: polarResult.customer?.name ?? undefined,\n63\t      polarCustomerId: polarResult.customerId ?? undefined,\n64\t    });\n65\t    if (!user) {\n66\t      return { success: false, error: \"Failed to get or create user\" };\n67\t    }\n68\t\n69\t    // 5. Insert license into database\n70\t    const license = await insertAccountKey({\n71\t      key: licenseKey,\n72\t      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n73\t      userId: user.id,\n74\t      polarLicenseKeyId: polarResult.id,\n75\t      polarCustomerId: polarResult.customerId ?? null,\n76\t      status: \"granted\",\n77\t    });\n78\t\n79\t    if (!license) {\n80\t      console.error(\"Failed to insert license from Polar\");\n81\t      return { success: false, error: \"Failed to import license\" };\n82\t    }\n83\t\n84\t    // 6. Create credit balance with 5000 credits\n85\t    try {\n86\t      await grantCreditLot({\n87\t        userId: license.userId,\n88\t        amount: 5000,\n89\t        sourceType: \"polar_bundle\",\n90\t        sourceId: polarResult.id,\n91\t      });\n92\t    } catch (creditError) {\n93\t      console.error(\"Failed to create credit balance:\", creditError);\n94\t      // License was created, but credits failed - still return success\n95\t    }\n96\t\n97\t    console.log(\n98\t      `Imported license from Polar: ${licenseKey} for ${email} with 5000 credits`\n99\t    );\n100\t\n101\t    return { success: true, licenseId: license.id };\n102\t  } catch (err) {\n103\t    console.error(\"Polar license import error:\", err);\n104\t    return {\n105\t      success: false,\n106\t      error: \"Failed to validate with Polar\",\n107\t    };\n108\t  }\n109\t}\n110\t\n111\texport type LicenseCheckResult =\n112\t  | { valid: true; license: AccountKeyRow }\n113\t  | { valid: false; error: string; status: number };\n114\t\n115\t/**\n116\t * Checks whether a license key is valid (exists and is \"granted\").\n117\t *\n118\t * 1. const polarImport = await importLicenseFromPolar(licenseKey.trim());\n131\t\n132\t    if (!polarImport.success) {\n133\t      return {\n134\t        valid: false,\n135\t        error: { polarClient, POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID } from \"@/lib/clients/polar\";\nimport {\n  findAccountByKey,\n  findAccountById,\n  findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId,\n  insertAccountKey,\n  grantCreditLot,\n  getOrCreateUser,\n  type AccountKeyRow,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * Shared license key validation logic.\n *\n * Used by both /api/license/validate and /api/license/activate so that\n * every entry point performs a real database check (with Polar fallback)\n * instead of trusting the key blindly.\n */\n\n/**\n * Import a license from Polar into the local database.\n *\n * Called when a license key is not found in the database but may exist in Polar.\n * Creates the license record, user (if needed), and grants 5000 credits.\n */\nasync function importLicenseFromPolar(licenseKey: string): Promise<{\n  success: boolean;\n  licenseId?: string;\n  error?: string;\n}> {\n  try {\n    // 1. Validate with Polar API\n    const polarResult = await polarClient.customerPortal.licenseKeys.validate({\n      key: licenseKey,\n      organizationId: POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID,\n    });\n\n    // 2. Check if valid\n    if (polarResult.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return { success: false, error: `License is ${polarResult.status}` };\n    }\n\n    // 2b. Dedupe by the stable Polar license-key id (casing-independent).\n    // findAccountByKey is a case-sensitive exact match, so a different casing\n    // or whitespace variant of an already-imported key misses the DB lookup\n    // and reaches this fallback. Without this guard each variant would insert a\n    // fresh license row and grant another 5000 credits. polarResult.id is the\n    // canonical resource id regardless of how the input key was cased.\n    const alreadyImported = await findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(polarResult.id);\n    if (alreadyImported) {\n      return { success: true, licenseId: alreadyImported.id };\n    }\n\n    // 3. Get customer email - required for import\n    const email = polarResult.customer?.email;\n    if (!email) {\n      return { success: false, error: \"License has no associated email\" };\n    }\n\n    // 4. Get or create user\n    const user = await getOrCreateUser(email, {\n      name: polarResult.customer?.name ?? undefined,\n      polarCustomerId: polarResult.customerId ?? undefined,\n    });\n    if (!user) {\n      return { success: false, error: \"Failed to get or create user\" };\n    }\n\n    // 5. Insert license into database\n    const license = await insertAccountKey({\n      key: licenseKey,\n      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n      userId: user.id,\n      polarLicenseKeyId: polarResult.id,\n      polarCustomerId: polarResult.customerId ?? null,\n      status: \"granted\",\n    });\n\n    if (!license) {\n      console.error(\"Failed to insert license from Polar\");\n      return { success: false, error: \"Failed to import license\" };\n    }\n\n    // 6. Create credit balance with 5000 credits\n    try {\n      await grantCreditLot({\n        userId: license.userId,\n        amount: 5000,\n        sourceType: \"polar_bundle\",\n        sourceId: polarResult.id,\n      });\n    } catch (creditError) {\n      console.error(\"Failed to create credit balance:\", creditError);\n      // License was created, but credits failed - still return success\n    }\n\n    console.log(\n      `Imported license from Polar: ${licenseKey} for ${email} with 5000 credits`\n    );\n\n    return { success: true, licenseId: license.id };\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"Polar license import error:\", err);\n    return {\n      success: false,\n      error: \"Failed to validate with Polar\",\n    };\n  }\n}\n\nexport type LicenseCheckResult =\n  | { valid: true; license: AccountKeyRow }\n  | { valid: false; error: string; status: number };\n\n/**\n * Checks whether a license key is valid (exists and is \"granted\").\n *\n * 1. Looks up the key in the database\n * 2. POLAR FALLBACK: if not found, validates against Polar and imports it\n * 3. Verifies the license status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n */\nexport async function checkLicenseKey(\n  licenseKey: string\n): Promise<LicenseCheckResult> {\n  // Query database for the license\n  let license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey.trim());\n\n  // POLAR FALLBACK: If not found in DB, try Polar API\n  if (!license) {\n    const polarImport = await importLicenseFromPolar(licenseKey.trim());\n\n    if (!polarImport.success) {\n      return {\n        valid: false,\n        error: polarImport.error || \"License key not found\",\n        status: 400,\n      };\n    }\n\n    // Re-query the newly imported license\n    license = await findAccountById(polarImport.licenseId!);\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return {\n        valid: false,\n        error: \"Failed to retrieve imported license\",\n        status: 500,\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Check status\n  if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n    return {\n      valid: false,\n      error: `License is ${license.status}`,\n      status: 400,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { valid: true, license };\n}\n","numLines":163,"startLine":1,"totalLines":163}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"366ae98a-0a9a-43a7-a0c9-cf800c10c486","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ createAuthEndpoint } from \"better-auth/api\";\nimport { setSessionCookie } from \"better-auth/cookies\";\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\n\nimport { findAccountByKey } from \"./db-layer\";\nimport { sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect } from \"./license-key-redirect\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { user } from \"@/src/db/schema/auth\";\n\nexport const licenseKeyPlugin = () => ({\n  id: \"license-key\",\n  endpoints: {\n    signInLicenseKey: createAuthEndpoint(\n      \"/sign-in/license-key\",\n      {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        requireHeaders: true,\n        body: z.object({\n          licenseKey: z.string(),\n          callbackURL: z.string().optional(),\n        }),\n      },\n      async (ctx) => {\n        const { licenseKey, callbackURL } = ctx.body;\n\n        const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n        if (!license || license.status !== \"granted\") {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Invalid or inactive license key.\" },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        if (!license.userId) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const [foundUser] = await db\n          .select()\n          .from(user)\n          .where(eq(user.id, license.userId))\n          .limit(1);\n\n        if (!foundUser) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const session = await ctx.context.internalAdapter.createSession(\n          foundUser.id,\n        );\n\n        if (!session) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Failed to create session.\" },\n            { status: 500 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        await setSessionCookie(ctx, { session, user: foundUser });\n\n        const ipAddress =\n          ctx.headers?.get(\"x-forwarded-for\")?.split(\",\")[0]?.trim() ??\n          \"unknown\";\n        const userAgent = ctx.headers?.get(\"user-agent\") ?? \"unknown\";\n        console.log(\n          `[license-key-sign-in] user=${foundUser.id} license=${license.id} ip=${ipAddress} ua=\"${userAgent}\"`,\n        );\n\n        return ctx.json({ redirect: sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL) });\n      },\n    ),\n  },\n  rateLimit: [\n    {\n      pathMatcher(path: string) {\n        return path === \"/sign-in/license-key\";\n      },\n      window: 60,\n      max: 5,\n    },\n  ],\n});\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"54782318-a8fb-48fc-a680-dc25b2bd605a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

'@/lib/clients/stripe';\n * const customers = await stripe.customers.list();\n * ```\n */\nexport const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {\n  // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments_preview is in private preview\n  apiVersion: \"2025-12-15.clover; managed_payments_preview=v1\",\n  typescript: true,\n});\n\n// Validate that required environment variables are set\nif (!process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY) {\n  console.warn(\n    \"STRIPE_SECRET_KEY not configured - Stripe operations will fail\"\n  );\n}\n=== polar\nimport { Polar } from \"@polar-sh/sdk\";\n\n// Initialize Polar SDK once and export for reuse\nexport const polarClient = new Polar({\n  server: process.env.NODE_ENV === \"production\" ? undefined : \"sandbox\",\n  accessToken: process.env.POLAR_ACCESS_TOKEN,\n});\n\n// Export organization ID for convenience\nexport const POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID = process.env.POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID || \"\";\n\n// Validate that required (!POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID) {\n  console.warn(\n    \"POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID not configured - license validation will fail\",\n  );\n}\n=== cloud redis 1-60\n// UPSTASH REDIS CLIENT\n// Serverless Redis for IP blocking and license caching\n// Works globally with Fly.io's anycast routing\n\nimport { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';\nimport { LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS } from './constants';\n\n// Initialize Redis client (lazy initialization for testing without Redis)\nlet _redis: Redis | null = null;\n\nfunction getRedis(): Redis {\n  if (!_redis) {\n    const url = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_URL;\n    const token = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN;\n\n    if (!url || !token) {\n      throw new Error('UPSTASH_REDIS_URL and UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN are required');\n    }\n\n    _redis = new Redis({ url, token });\n  }\n  return _redis;\n}\n\n// Export redis getter for lazy initialization\nexport const redis = {\n  get: getRedis,\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// IP BLOCKING + DAILY QUOTA (credits-based)\n// ============================================================================\n\nexport async function isIPBlocked(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    const blockKey = `ip_blocked:${ip}`;\n    const blocked = await getRedis().get(blockKey);\n    return blocked === 'true';\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// LICENSE CACHE (1 hour TTL for valid + invalid)\n// ============================================================================\n\nexport interface CachedLicense {\n  isValid: boolean;\n  credits: number;\n  cachedAt: string;\n}\n\nexport async function getCachedLicense(licenseKey: string): Promise<CachedLicense | null> {\n  try {\n    const cached = await getRedis().get<CachedLicense>(`license:${licenseKey}`);\n    if (!cached) return null;\n\n    if (typeof cached === 'string') {\n      return JSON.parse(cached) as []).map((m) => ({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id })));\n}\n\nconst PROVIDERS = [\n  { name: \"openai\",    envKey: \"OPENAI_API_KEY\",    fetcher: fetchOpenAI },\n  { name: \"anthropic\", envKey: \"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\", fetcher: fetchAnthropic },\n  { name: \"gemini\",    envKey: \"GEMINI_API_KEY\",    fetcher: fetchGemini },\n  { name: \"groq\",      envKey: \"GROQ_API_KEY\",      fetcher: fetchGroq },\n  { name: \"grok\",      envKey: \"XAI_API_KEY\",       fetcher: fetchXAI },\n  { name: \"cerebras\",  envKey: \"CEREBRAS_API_KEY\",  fetcher: fetchCerebras },\n] as const;\n\nexport type AvailableModels = {\n  fetchedAt: string;\n  providers: Record<string, ProviderResult>;\n};\n\nasync function fetchAvailableModelsUncached(): Promise<AvailableModels> {\n  const entries = await Promise.all(\n    PROVIDERS.map(async ({ name, envKey, fetcher }): Promise<[string, ProviderResult]> => {\n      const key = process.env[envKey];\n      if (!key) return [name, { ok: false, error: `missing ${envKey}` }];\n      try {\n        const models = await fetcher(key);\n        return [name, { ok: true, models }];\n      } catch (e) {\n        return [name, { ok: false, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }];\n      }\n    })\n  );\n\n  return {\n    fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    providers: Object.fromEntries(entries),\n  };\n}\n\n// Per-instance in-memory cache (~1h TTL). Bounds the 6-provider fan-out even\n// against cache-busting query strings, which bypass Vercel's edge cache and\n// hit the function directly. In-flight de-duplication collapses concurrent\n// cold-start requests into a single upstream fan-out.\nconst CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;\n// If every provider failed (e.g. a transient cold-start network blip), cache\n// only briefly so a fully-broken response isn't pinned for the full hour.\nconst FAILURE_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 1000;","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"ce2d0db9-2c3c-41d3-b6c3-d645682b6a5f","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:18.226Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"    const id = m.id.toLowerCase();\n    if (XAI_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => id.includes(ex))) continue;\n    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n  }\n  return sortModels(models);\n}\n\nasync function fetchCerebras(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.cerebras.ai/v1/models\", {\n    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n  return sortModels((data.data ?? []).map((m) => ({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id })));\n}\n\nconst PROVIDERS = [\n  { name: \"openai\",    envKey: \"OPENAI_API_KEY\",    fetcher: fetchOpenAI },\n  { name: \"anthropic\", envKey: \"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\", fetcher: fetchAnthropic },\n  { name: \"gemini\",    envKey: \"GEMINI_API_KEY\",    fetcher: fetchGemini },\n  { name: \"groq\",      envKey: \"GROQ_API_KEY\",      fetcher: fetchGroq },\n  { name: \"grok\",      envKey: \"XAI_API_KEY\",       fetcher: fetchXAI },\n  { name: \"cerebras\",  envKey: \"CEREBRAS_API_KEY\",  fetcher: fetchCerebras },\n] as const;\n\nexport type AvailableModels = {\n  fetchedAt: string;\n  providers: Record<string, ProviderResult>;\n};\n\nasync function fetchAvailableModelsUncached(): Promise<AvailableModels> {\n  const entries = await Promise.all(\n    PROVIDERS.map(async ({ name, envKey, fetcher }): Promise<[string, ProviderResult]> => {\n      const key = process.env[envKey];\n      if (!key) return [name, { ok: false, error: `missing ${envKey}` }];\n      try {\n        const models = await fetcher(key);\n        return [name, { ok: true, models }];\n      } catch (e) {\n        return [name, { ok: false, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }];\n      }\n    })\n  );\n\n  return {\n    fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    providers: Object.fromEntries(entries),\n  };\n}\n\n// Per-instance in-memory cache (~1h TTL). Bounds the 6-provider fan-out even\n// against cache-busting query strings, which bypass Vercel's edge cache and\n// hit the function directly. In-flight de-duplication collapses concurrent\n// cold-start requests into a single upstream fan-out.\nconst CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;\n// If every provider failed (e.g. a transient cold-start network blip), cache\n// only briefly so a fully-broken response isn't pinned for the full hour.\nconst FAILURE_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 1000;","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"42d8099d-73a6-4423-90b1-952e938e25c5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"d0bc4249-fb3b-4fff-be7d-74fea3c08209","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a22e9d5e8089de9e6","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWJ7A59LokigxCUh3nvH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Sweep complete. I9 holds almost everywhere — this is a genuinely clean surface. Three low/medium candidates, and several of the suspected sites refute.\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `nextjs/lib/security/timing-safe-secret.ts`\nLine: 13\nInvariant: I9\nClaim: The early `if (receivedBuffer.length !== expectedBuffer.length) return false;` short-circuits before `timingSafeEqual`, so the comparison is constant-time only *within* a length class — response latency distinguishes a correct-length guess from a wrong-length one.\nFailure scenario: An attacker POSTs `/api/internal/grant-license` with `x-internal-secret` headers of increasing byte length. Requests whose length matches `HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET` take a measurably longer path (TextEncoder alloc + `timingSafeEqual` over N bytes) than mismatched-length ones, which return at line 13. With enough samples over a Vercel function this reveals the exact secret length, collapsing the offline brute-force space for a header that mints Account Keys and 5000-credit grants at `/api/internal/grant-license`. The standard fix is to HMAC or SHA-256 both operands to a fixed width before comparing, so no branch depends on the secret's length. Same leak applies to the HMAC hex compare at `add-blog-post/route.ts:81`.\nConfidence: medium\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/redis.ts`\nLine: 34\nInvariant: I9\nClaim: `isIPBlocked` wraps `getRedis().get(...)` in a bare `catch { return false }`, and `getRedis()` *throws* when `UPSTASH_REDIS_URL`/`UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN` are unset — so a missing env credential turns the IP blocklist into a silent no-op rather than failing closed.\nFailure scenario: An operator blocks an abusive IP by writing `ip_blocked:<ip>` in Upstash. A Fly machine that boots without `UPSTASH_REDIS_URL`/`UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN` (a fresh region, a rotated-secret deploy, or an Upstash outage) has every `isIPBlocked` call return `false` with **no log line at all** — the catch is empty. The blocked IP regains full access to `/transcribe`, `/post-process`, `/assistant` and `/ws/streaming-deepgram`, and since the license-key auth path still works from Redis-less code (it falls straight through to the Next.js API), the abuse resumes at full paid-provider cost. Operators see the block in the Upstash console and believe it is in force.\nConfidence: low (framing caveat: the compared value is a Redis flag, not an env secret; it is the \"fails closed when the env var is unset\" clause that is violated, not the comparison clause)\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `nextjs/app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts`\nLine: 259\nInvariant: I9\nClaim: When `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` *is* set, auth is `isSignatureValid(...) || isBearerAuthorized(req)` — the static bearer token remains a fully equivalent credential, so enabling HMAC signing adds no security over the bearer path it was meant to harden.\nFailure scenario: An operator, believing the endpoint is now payload-bound, sets `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` and leaves `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` in place (the schema makes it `z.string().min(1)`, i.e. mandatory, so it cannot be removed without failing boot validation). A token leaked from CI logs or the Outrank dashboard still authorizes an arbitrary `publish_articles` payload: the attacker's articles are upserted into `blog_posts` and rendered at `app/[locale]/blog/[slug]/page.tsx:170` via `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` on attacker-supplied `content_html`. The signing secret provides zero mitigation.\nConfidence: low (the file comments at lines 19-24 and 253-256 document this fallback as deliberate, so this is an \"advertised protection is inert\" finding rather than a fail-open on an unset env var)\n\n### Coverage\n\n**Examined buckets:** B1 (cloud-entry-and-auth — `middleware/auth.ts`, `middleware/credits.ts`, `lib/redis.ts`, `index.ts`), B2 (cloud-routes — all five, incl. the Deepgram WS key guard), B3 (all 11 STT adapters' key guards), B4 (all 7 LLM adapters' key guards), B5 (`google-auth.ts`, `gcs-storage.ts`, `scripts/smoke-test.ts` env), B6 (both webhooks read in full; `license/*`, `account/*`, `checkout/*` grepped and confirmed to contain **no** secret/authorization check of any kind), B7 (all three gated `/api/internal/*` routes, `internal/models`, `middleware.ts`, `timing-safe-secret.ts`, `rate-limit.ts`, `license-validation.ts`, `auth-license-key-plugin.ts`), B8 (`server/api/trpc.ts` admin gates), B9 (`clients/{stripe,polar,redis,resend}.ts`, `model-list.ts`, `geolocation.ts`, `stripe-webhook.ts` mint sites), B10/B11/B12 (swept by an exhaustive `env.[A-Z_]+` inventory across `components/`, `contexts/`, `config/`, `tests/`, `i18n.ts` — only `NEXT_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_*` and one test reference; no credential comparison exists in these buckets).\n\n**Not examined / partially examined:** B10/B11/B12 were covered by grep inventory rather than file-by-file reading — justified because I9 requires a credential↔env-secret comparison, and the exhaustive env-reference inventory shows no secret-shaped env var is referenced anywhere in those 78 files.\n\n**Leads refuted:**\n- **All three `/api/internal/*` routes** — verified: `grant-license:11`, `licenses-for-email:11`, `granted-emails:14` each call `timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)` and `return 401` *before* any `req.json()` or DB call. `HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET` is `z.string().optional()`, so an unset value does not fail boot — but the helper's `!expected` guard makes every request 401, i.e. fail-closed (the ACS integration breaks loudly rather than opening).\n- **`add-blog-post` missing-env matrix** — all four combinations fail closed. Signing unset + token unset ⇒ `isBearerAuthorized` returns `false` at line 87 ⇒ 401 before the body is even buffered. Signing set + token unset ⇒ HMAC becomes the sole path, and `isSignatureValid` returns `false` on a missing secret. A missing `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` never leaves `timingSafeEqualSecret` as the only barrier — it removes the bearer path entirely. Only the \"inert signing secret\" nuance above survives.\n- **`webhooks/stripe/route.ts:33-40`** — confirmed correct (500 on missing `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, 400 on missing signature header). No sibling handler does the opposite: `add-blog-post` is the only other webhook and it 401s. Grepped for a fail-open twin across all 23 route handlers; none exists.\n- **Cloud `x-internal` / admin header comparison** — does not exist. `hyperwhisper-cloud` has zero internal/admin headers; the only credential it consumes is the license key, which it forwards to `/api/license/validate`. Nothing on the cloud side compares a caller value to an env secret.\n- **Missing provider API key ⇒ silent auth skip** — refuted for all 18 adapter entry points. Every one guards `if (!apiKey) throw` (STT adapters and `anthropic.ts`), or throws a tagged 503 error (`openai-llm`, `gemini-llm`, `xai-llm`, `mistral-llm`), or `ProviderUnavailableError` (`xai-stt`), or a per-region throw (`azure-mai:115`). `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` reads `process.env.DEEPGRAM_API_KEY || ''` but checks `if (!apiKey)` in `onOpen` and closes 1011 before constructing the upstream socket. `google-auth.ts:34` and `model-list.ts:161` also fail closed.\n- **Any `===` credential comparison** — none exists anywhere in the 210-file scope. `grep -riE \"(secret|token|apikey|password|signature|bearer|credential)\\w*\\s*(===|!==|==)\"` and `grep -riE \"(===|!==)\\s*(process\\.)?env\\.[A-Z_]+\"` both return zero credential hits across both services. Every env-secret comparison routes through `timingSafeEqualSecret` or `stripe.webhooks.constructEvent`.\n- **`DEV_BYPASS_KEY`** — declared in `src/env/schema.mjs:42` and documented as \"skips license validation\", but referenced by **no code**. There is no dev-bypass path to open.\n\n**Adjacent, belongs to another finder:** `nextjs/app/api/internal/models/route.ts` has **no** `x-internal-secret` gate despite sitting under the `/api/internal/` prefix where its three siblings all enforce one; any anonymous caller can trigger the 6-provider paid fan-out on a cold lambda. There is no broken comparison here (none was ever written), so it is I7's unauthenticated-amplification finding, not I9's. Likewise `HYPERWHISPER_CLOUD_API_KEY` is declared in the schema and documented as \"for CF Workers to call credits endpoints\" but is enforced by no route — `/api/license/credits` accepts the deducting POST on the license key alone (I6/I7)."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1056,"cache_read_input_tokens":99348,"output_tokens":3514,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1056},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3514,"cache_read_input_tokens":99348,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1056,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1056,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWJ7846SeGeDjCUVpX1D","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"6cd949db-8a6b-4538-be8c-32e02df20c85","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:48:48.730Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
